JavaScript 复制

```javascript
if(sp.isInf){
```

This heuristic for "circle encloses infinity" is weird. A circle encloses infinity if the origin is outside the circle? Actually in complex analysis, a contour encloses ∞ if it's a large circle around the origin. The condition `circle.r > 5 && distFromOrigin < circle.r * 0.5` means the circle is large and the origin is within half the radius. This is a rough heuristic but not mathematically precise. A better condition would be `circle.r > 5 && distFromOrigin + some_threshold < circle.r` or just `circle.r > 5 && distFromOrigin < circle.r`. 收到。这是一个**复平面网格变形与柯西积分教学**的交互式 HTML 工具，功能包括：

- 13 种复变函数的网格映射可视化（全纯、亚纯、本性奇点、分支点等）

- 两种工作模式：柯西定理（∮f(z)dz）与柯西积分公式（∮f(z)/(z-a)dz）
- 动画过渡、双指缩放/平移、奇点标记、分支切割线等

我在代码中发现了几处需要关注的问题：

---

### 1. `sinc` 函数映射的括号缺失（数学错误）

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
// 当前代码（第 ~340 行）
return {x:sz.x*z.x+sz.y*z.y/(d*d), y:(sz.y*z.x-sz.x*z.y)/(d*d)};
```

实部缺少括号，导致 `sz.y*z.y` 没有除以 `d²`。应改为：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
return {x:(sz.x*z.x+sz.y*z.y)/(d*d), y:(sz.y*z.x-sz.x*z.y)/(d*d)};
```

### 2. 柯西积分公式成功时的结果对比缺失

在 `updateIntegralFollowerFinalFormula` 中，当围道**正确包围 a 且无奇点**时（`enclosesA && !hasOtherSingularity`），`if` 块为空，没有显示 `结果 ≈ f(a)` 的对比。这是用户最想看到的验证效果。

### 3. 动画结束后积分面板不跟随视图

动画完成后，如果用户平移/缩放画布，积分面板仍停留在屏幕绝对坐标，不会跟随围道终点移动。

### 4. `Residue` / `Taylor` / `Laurent` 三个模式为占位符

目前仅切换了提示文字，没有实现留数计算、级数展开可视化等实际功能。

Actually, for the residue theorem, we usually consider finite poles. The residue at infinity is tricky. For now, let's only compute residues at finite poles.For `z2p1` (z²+1), there are no finite poles, only pole at infinity. The residue theorem in its basic form applies to finite poles. So if a contour doesn't enclose any finite singularity, ∮f(z)dz = 0.For `z_plus_invz`, finite pole at z=0 with Res = 1.For `sinc` (sin(z)/z), removable singularity at 0, residue is 0.For `exp_invz` and `sin_invz`, essential singularity at 0. }

For numerical derivatives:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function derivative(func, a, order){
  const h = 1e-4;
  if(order === 1){
    const f1 = func({x: a.x + h, y: a.y});
    const f2 = func({x: a.x - h, y: a.y});
    return {x: (f1.x - f2.x)/(2*h), y: (f1.y - f2.y)/(2*h)};
  }
  // ... higher orders
}
```

But complex derivatives require Cauchy-Riemann. Numerical differentiation in complex plane is tricky.Simpler: for each supported function, hardcode the Taylor series.

Add `updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue`:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
  function updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue(photonScreen, re, im, enclosed){
    let valStr = formatComplex({x:re, y:im}, 2);
    let html = '<div style=\"font-size:13px; font-style:italic; white-space:nowrap;\">';\n    html += '<span style=\"font-size:18px; font-style:italic;\">∮</span><span class=\"int-c\">C</span><span style=\"font-style:italic;\">f(z)dz</span> = ' + valStr;\n    html += '</div>';
    
    if(enclosed && enclosed.length > 0){
      let sumRes = {x:0, y:0};
      let resDetails = [];
      for(const sp of enclosed){
        const res = computeResidue(funcType, sp);
        if(res){
          sumRes.x += res.x;
          sumRes.y += res.y;
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=' + formatComplex(res, 2));
        } else {
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=∞/unknown');
        }
      }
      const twoPiIRes = {x: -2*Math.PI*sumRes.y, y: 2*Math.PI*sumRes.x};
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:4px; color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(sumRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style=\"color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">2πi·ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(twoPiIRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:3px; color:#aaa; font-size:10px; font-family:monospace;\">' + resDetails.join(' | ') + '</div>';
    } else {
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:4px; color:#4ade80; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">No poles inside C → ΣRes = 0</div>';
    }
    integralFormula.innerHTML = html;
    integralValue.textContent = '';
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
  }
```

```javascript
      case 'const1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = 1', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'identity': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = a + (z-a)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z2p1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = (a²+1) + 2a(z-a) + (z-a)²', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z': 
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent: z⁻¹', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: 'f(z) = Σ (-1)ⁿ a⁻ⁿ⁻¹ (z-a)ⁿ', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z2':
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent: z⁻²', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: 'f(z) = Σ (n+1)(-1)ⁿ a⁻ⁿ⁻² (z-a)ⁿ', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z_plus_invz':
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent: z + z⁻¹', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: 'f(z) = z + Σ (-1)ⁿ a⁻ⁿ⁻¹ (z-a)ⁿ', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sinc': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'Taylor at 0: 1 - z²/3! + z⁴/5! - ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = eᵃ Σ (z-a)ⁿ/n!', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sin_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = sin(a) + cos(a)(z-a) - ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_invz':
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent: 1 + z⁻¹ + z⁻²/2! + ...', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'sin_invz':
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent: z⁻¹ - z⁻³/3! + ...', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'log_z':
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no Laurent', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: 'ln(a) + Σ (-1)ⁿ⁺¹ (z-a)ⁿ/(n aⁿ)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sqrt_z':
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no Laurent', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: '√a + (z-a)/(2√a) - ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'cuberoot_z':
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.y) < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no Laurent', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: Math.hypot(a.x, a.y), series: 'a^(1/3) + (z-a)/(3a^(2/3)) - ...', type: 'taylor'};
    }
  }
```

computeResidue function:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
  function computeResidue(funcType, sp){
    if(sp.type === 'removable') return {x:0, y:0};
    if(sp.type === 'branch') return null;
    if(sp.type === 'essential'){
      if(funcType === 'exp_invz' && sp.label === 'z=0') return {x:1, y:0};
      if(funcType === 'sin_invz' && sp.label === 'z=0') return {x:1, y:0};
      return null;
    }
    if(sp.type === 'pole'){
      switch(funcType){
        case 'inv_z': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:1, y:0} : null;
        case 'inv_z2': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:0, y:0} : null;
        case 'z_plus_invz': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:1, y:0} : null;
        case 'z2p1': return {x:0, y:0};
        case 'sinc': return {x:0, y:0};
        default: return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
```

updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
  function updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue(photonScreen, re, im, enclosed){
    let valStr = formatComplex({x:re, y:im}, 2);
    let html = '<div style=\"font-size:13px; font-style:italic; white-space:nowrap;\">';
    html += '<span style=\"font-size:18px; font-style:italic;\">∮</span><span class=\"int-c\">C</span><span style=\"font-style:italic;\">f(z)dz</span> = ' + valStr;
    html += '</div>';
    
    if(enclosed && enclosed.length > 0){
      let sumRes = {x:0, y:0};
      let resDetails = [];
      for(const sp of enclosed){
        const res = computeResidue(funcType, sp);
        if(res){
          sumRes.x += res.x;
          sumRes.y += res.y;
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=' + formatComplex(res, 2));
        } else {
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=∞/unknown');
        }
      }
      const twoPiIRes = {x: -2*Math.PI*sumRes.y, y: 2*Math.PI*sumRes.x};
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:4px; color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(sumRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style=\"color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">2πi·ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(twoPiIRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:3px; color:#aaa; font-size:10px; font-family:monospace;\">' + resDetails.join(' | ') + '</div>';
    } else {
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:4px; color:#4ade80; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">No poles inside C → ΣRes = 0</div>';
    }
    integralFormula.innerHTML = html;
    integralValue.textContent = '';
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
  }
```

getConvergenceInfo:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
  function getConvergenceInfo(funcType, a){
    const ra = Math.hypot(a.x, a.y);
    switch(funcType){
      case 'const1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = 1', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'identity': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = a + (z−a)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z2p1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = (a²+1) + 2a(z−a) + (z−a)²', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z': 
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−1</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = Σ (−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−1</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z2':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−2</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = Σ (n+1)(−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−2</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z_plus_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z + z<sup>−1</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = z + Σ (−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−1</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sinc': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'Taylor at 0: 1 − z²/3! + z⁴/5! − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = e<sup>a</sup> · Σ (z−a)<sup>n</sup>/n!', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sin_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = sin(a) + cos(a)(z−a) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: Σ z<sup>−n</sup>/n!', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'sin_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−1</sup> − z<sup>−3</sup>/3! + ...', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'log_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'ln(a) + Σ (−1)<sup>n+1</sup>(z−a)<sup>n</sup>/(n·a<sup>n</sup>)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sqrt_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: '√a + (z−a)/(2√a) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'cuberoot_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'a<sup>1/3</sup> + (z−a)/(3a<sup>2/3</sup>) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      default: return {radius: 0, series: 'Unknown', type: 'none'};
    }
  }
```

Insert before `function drawCircle(){`:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
  function computeResidue(funcType, sp){
    if(sp.type === 'removable') return {x:0, y:0};
    if(sp.type === 'branch') return null;
    if(sp.type === 'essential'){
      if(funcType === 'exp_invz' && sp.label === 'z=0') return {x:1, y:0};
      if(funcType === 'sin_invz' && sp.label === 'z=0') return {x:1, y:0};
      return null;
    }
    if(sp.type === 'pole'){
      switch(funcType){
        case 'inv_z': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:1, y:0} : null;
        case 'inv_z2': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:0, y:0} : null;
        case 'z_plus_invz': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:1, y:0} : null;
        case 'z2p1': return {x:0, y:0};
        case 'sinc': return {x:0, y:0};
        default: return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
```

**Mod 5: updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue**
Insert before `function drawGrid(){`:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
  function updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue(photonScreen, re, im, enclosed){
    let valStr = formatComplex({x:re, y:im}, 2);
    let html = '<div style=\"font-size:13px; font-style:italic; white-space:nowrap;\">';
    html += '<span style=\"font-size:18px; font-style:italic;\">∮</span><span class=\"int-c\">C</span><span style=\"font-style:italic;\">f(z)dz</span> = ' + valStr;
    html += '</div>';
    if(enclosed && enclosed.length > 0){
      let sumRes = {x:0, y:0};
      let resDetails = [];
      for(const sp of enclosed){
        const res = computeResidue(funcType, sp);
        if(res){
          sumRes.x += res.x;
          sumRes.y += res.y;
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=' + formatComplex(res, 2));
        } else {
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=∞/unknown');
        }
      }
      const twoPiIRes = {x: -2*Math.PI*sumRes.y, y: 2*Math.PI*sumRes.x};
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:4px; color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(sumRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style=\"color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">2πi·ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(twoPiIRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:3px; color:#aaa; font-size:10px; font-family:monospace;\">' + resDetails.join(' | ') + '</div>';
    } else {
      html += '<div style=\"margin-top:4px; color:#4ade80; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;\">No poles inside C → ΣRes = 0</div>';
    }
    integralFormula.innerHTML = html;
    integralValue.textContent = '';
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
  }
```

**Mod 6: getConvergenceInfo**
Insert before `function drawGrid(){`:

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
  function getConvergenceInfo(funcType, a){
    const ra = Math.hypot(a.x, a.y);
    switch(funcType){
      case 'const1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = 1', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'identity': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = a + (z−a)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z2p1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = (a²+1) + 2a(z−a) + (z−a)²', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z': 
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−1</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = Σ (−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−1</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z2':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−2</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = Σ (n+1)(−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−2</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z_plus_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z + z<sup>−1</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = z + Σ (−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−1</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sinc': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'Taylor at 0: 1 − z²/3! + z⁴/5! − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = e<sup>a</sup> · Σ (z−a)<sup>n</sup>/n!', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sin_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = sin(a) + cos(a)(z−a) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: Σ z<sup>−n</sup>/n!', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'sin_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−1</sup> − z<sup>−3</sup>/3! + ...', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'log_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'ln(a) + Σ (−1)<sup>n+1</sup>(z−a)<sup>n</sup>/(n·a<sup>n</sup>)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sqrt_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: '√a + (z−a)/(2√a) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'cuberoot_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'a<sup>1/3</sup> + (z−a)/(3a<sup>2/3</sup>) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      default: return {radius: 0, series: 'Unknown', type: 'none'};
    }
  }
```
