我无法生成外部托管的预览链接（系统限制不支持创建可下载 HTML 文件）。但我已经把全部修复整合好了，你可以直接复制下面的代码，保存为 `.html` 文件后用浏览器打开——浏览器窗口就是「预览」，右键「查看网页源代码」就是「代码」。修复内容：

1. **`sinc` 函数括号缺失** — 实部现在正确除以 `d²`
2. **Cauchy 公式成功时显示 `f(a)` 对比** — 积分结果旁会显示 `f(a)` 值与误差
3. **动画结束后面板跟随视图** — 平移/缩放时积分面板自动跟随光点位置（你仍可手动拖动）
4. **Residue 模式实现** — 画圆包围极点后，动画计算 ∮f(z)dz，并对比 `2πi·ΣRes`
5. **Taylor / Laurent 模式实现** — 点击放置展开中心，显示级数公式与收敛半径圆

下面是完整修复后的代码：

HTML 预览 复制

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no, maximum-scale=1.0">
<title>复平面网格变形 — 柯西积分教学</title>
<style>
  * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
  html, body {
    width: 100%; height: 100%; overflow: hidden;
    background: #000; touch-action: none; overscroll-behavior: none;
    -webkit-touch-callout: none; -webkit-user-select: none; user-select: none;
  }
  #app {
    width: 100%; height: 100%; position: relative;
    overflow: hidden; touch-action: none; overscroll-behavior: none;
  }
  canvas {
    display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%;
    touch-action: none; overscroll-behavior: none;
  }
  .func-label {
    position: absolute; bottom: 10px; left: 10px;
    pointer-events: none;
    font-family: 'Times New Roman', Georgia, 'Cambria Math', serif;
    font-size: 18px; font-style: italic; color: #0ff;
    opacity: 0.9; letter-spacing: 0.3px;
    text-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(0,255,200,0.25);
    line-height: 1.4;
  }
  .func-label .var { font-style: italic; }
  .func-label sup {
    font-size: 0.55em; vertical-align: 0.7em; margin-left: 1px;
    font-style: italic;
  }
  .func-label .eq { margin: 0 2px; }
  .func-label .paren { margin: 0 1px; }
  .func-label .minus { margin: 0 1px; }
  .func-label .dot { margin: 0 1px; }

  .btn {
    position: absolute; top: 12px;
    background: rgba(0,255,200,0.08); border: 1px solid rgba(0,255,200,0.35);
    color: #0ff; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;
    padding: 6px 14px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px;
    touch-action: none; transition: background 0.15s;
  }
  .btn:active { background: rgba(0,255,200,0.25); }
  .btn-reset { right: 12px; }
  .btn-animate { right: 80px; }

  .mode-switch {
    position: absolute; bottom: 10px; right: 10px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1px;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
    border: 1px solid rgba(0,255,200,0.25);
    border-radius: 4px;
    overflow: hidden;
    z-index: 100;
  }
  .mode-switch .mode-btn {
    padding: 5px 10px;
    font-family: monospace; font-size: 10px;
    color: rgba(0,255,200,0.5);
    background: transparent;
    border: none;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    white-space: nowrap;
    text-align: left;
  }
  .mode-switch .mode-btn.active {
    color: #0ff;
    background: rgba(0,255,200,0.15);
    font-weight: bold;
  }

  .custom-select {
    position: absolute; top: 12px; left: 12px;
    z-index: 100; user-select: none;
  }
  .select-trigger {
    font-family: 'Times New Roman', Georgia, serif;
    font-size: 14px; font-style: italic; color: #0ff;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.6); border: 1px solid rgba(0,255,200,0.35);
    padding: 6px 28px 6px 12px; border-radius: 4px;
    min-width: 140px; cursor: pointer; position: relative;
  }
  .select-trigger .arrow {
    position: absolute; right: 8px; top: 50%;
    transform: translateY(-50%); font-size: 10px; font-style: normal;
  }
  .select-options {
    display: none; position: absolute; top: 100%; left: 0;
    margin-top: 4px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.92);
    border: 1px solid rgba(0,255,200,0.35); border-radius: 4px;
    overflow: hidden; min-width: 200px; max-height: 70vh; overflow-y: auto;
  }
  .select-options.active { display: block; }
  .select-options div {
    padding: 8px 12px; cursor: pointer;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0,255,200,0.1);
    font-family: 'Times New Roman', Georgia, serif;
    font-size: 13px; font-style: italic; color: #0ff;
  }
  .select-options div:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
  .select-options div:hover { background: rgba(0,255,200,0.15); }
  .select-options .cat-green { color: #4ade80; }
  .select-options .cat-yellow { color: #facc15; }
  .select-options .cat-red { color: #f87171; }

  #infoPanel {
    position: absolute; top: 48px; left: 12px;
    display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
  #categoryLabel {
    font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;
    padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 3px;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
  }
  #singularityLabel {
    font-family: monospace; font-size: 11px;
    color: rgba(255,50,50,0.95);
    padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 3px;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
    max-width: 320px; line-height: 1.5;
  }

  #integralFollower {
    display: none;
    position: absolute;
    pointer-events: none;
    background: transparent;
    border: none;
    padding: 8px;
    color: #0ff;
    font-family: 'Times New Roman', Georgia, serif;
    font-size: 15px;
    white-space: nowrap;
    z-index: 60;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: default;
  }
  #integralFollower.drag-enabled {
    pointer-events: auto;
    cursor: grab;
  }
  #integralFollower.drag-enabled:active {
    cursor: grabbing;
  }
  #integralFormula {
    font-style: italic;
    margin-bottom: 3px;
    font-size: 15px;
    letter-spacing: 0.3px;
  }
  #integralFormula .int-c {
    margin-left: -2px;
    margin-right: 1px;
    font-size: 0.65em;
    vertical-align: -0.3em;
  }
  #integralValue {
    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
    font-size: 13px;
    font-weight: bold;
  }
  .integral-green { color: #4ade80; }
  .integral-red { color: #f87171; }
  .integral-white { color: #fff; }

  #flashPanel {
    display: none;
    position: absolute;
    top: 50%; left: 50%;
    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.85);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,80,80,0.6);
    border-radius: 10px;
    padding: 20px 28px;
    color: #ff6b6b;
    font-family: 'Times New Roman', Georgia, serif;
    font-size: 16px;
    text-align: center;
    max-width: 340px;
    line-height: 1.6;
    z-index: 200;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
  #flashPanel .flash-title {
    font-size: 20px; font-weight: bold;
    margin-bottom: 10px;
    color: #ff4444;
  }
  #flashPanel .flash-reason {
    font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
    font-size: 13px;
    color: #ff9999;
    margin-top: 8px;
  }

  #hintLabel {
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 52px; left: 10px;
    transform: none;
    font-family: monospace;
    font-size: 11px;
    color: rgba(0,255,200,0.55);
    pointer-events: none;
    text-align: left;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
    padding: 4px 10px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    max-width: 60%;
  }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="app">
  <canvas id="c"></canvas>
  <div class="func-label" id="funcLabel"></div>

  <div class="custom-select" id="funcSelectWrapper">
    <div class="select-trigger" id="funcTrigger">
      <span id="triggerText"></span>
      <span class="arrow">▼</span>
    </div>
    <div class="select-options" id="funcOptions">
      <div data-value="const1" class="cat-green">1&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🟢 全纯-整函-单值</div>
      <div data-value="identity" class="cat-green"><i>z</i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🟢 全纯-整函-单值</div>
      <div data-value="z2p1" class="cat-yellow"><i>z</i><sup>2</sup>+1&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🟡 亚纯-∞极点-有理函-单值</div>
      <div data-value="inv_z" class="cat-yellow">1/<i>z</i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🟡 亚纯-有限远极点-有理函-单值</div>
      <div data-value="inv_z2" class="cat-yellow">1/<i>z</i><sup>2</sup>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🟡 亚纯-有限远极点-有理函-单值</div>
      <div data-value="z_plus_invz" class="cat-yellow"><i>z</i>+1/<i>z</i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🟡 亚纯-混合极点-有理函-单值</div>
      <div data-value="sinc" class="cat-yellow">sin(<i>z</i>)/<i>z</i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🟡 亚纯-有限远可去-单值</div>
      <div data-value="exp_z" class="cat-red"><i>e</i><sup><i>z</i></sup>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🔴 非亚纯-∞本性奇点-单值</div>
      <div data-value="sin_z" class="cat-red">sin(<i>z</i>)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🔴 非亚纯-∞本性奇点-单值</div>
      <div data-value="exp_invz" class="cat-red"><i>e</i><sup>1/<i>z</i></sup>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🔴 非亚纯-有限远本性奇点-单值</div>
      <div data-value="sin_invz" class="cat-red">sin(1/<i>z</i>)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🔴 非亚纯-有限远本性奇点-单值</div>
      <div data-value="log_z" class="cat-red">ln(<i>z</i>)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🔴 非亚纯-分支点-多值</div>
      <div data-value="sqrt_z" class="cat-red">√<i>z</i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🔴 非亚纯-分支点-多值</div>
      <div data-value="cuberoot_z" class="cat-red"><i>z</i><sup>1/3</sup>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;🔴 非亚纯-分支点-多值</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div id="infoPanel">
    <div id="categoryLabel"></div>
    <div id="singularityLabel"></div>
  </div>

  <button class="btn btn-animate" id="animBtn">Animate</button>
  <button class="btn btn-reset" id="resetBtn">Reset</button>

  <div class="mode-switch" id="modeSwitch">
    <button class="mode-btn active" data-mode="cauchy-theorem">Cauchy Thm</button>
    <button class="mode-btn" data-mode="cauchy-formula">Cauchy Formula</button>
    <button class="mode-btn" data-mode="residue">Residue Thm</button>
    <button class="mode-btn" data-mode="taylor">Taylor Series</button>
    <button class="mode-btn" data-mode="laurent">Laurent Series</button>
  </div>

  <div id="integralFollower">
    <div id="integralFormula"></div>
    <div id="integralValue"></div>
  </div>

  <div id="flashPanel">
    <div class="flash-title">⚠ Not Analytic Inside</div>
    <div id="flashText"></div>
    <div class="flash-reason" id="flashReason"></div>
  </div>

  <div id="hintLabel">Drag on grid to draw a circle → auto computes ∮<sub>C</sub>f(z)dz</div>
</div>
<script>
(function(){

  const canvas = document.getElementById('c');
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetBtn');
  const animBtn = document.getElementById('animBtn');
  const funcTrigger = document.getElementById('funcTrigger');
  const funcOptions = document.getElementById('funcOptions');
  const triggerText = document.getElementById('triggerText');
  const funcLabel = document.getElementById('funcLabel');
  const categoryLabel = document.getElementById('categoryLabel');
  const singularityLabel = document.getElementById('singularityLabel');
  const integralFollower = document.getElementById('integralFollower');
  const integralFormula = document.getElementById('integralFormula');
  const integralValue = document.getElementById('integralValue');
  const flashPanel = document.getElementById('flashPanel');
  const flashText = document.getElementById('flashText');
  const flashReason = document.getElementById('flashReason');
  const hintLabel = document.getElementById('hintLabel');
  const modeSwitch = document.getElementById('modeSwitch');
  const colors = ['#E60000','#FF7F00','#FFD700','#FF69B4','#00CC66','#87CEEB','#0033CC','#4B0082'];

  let W, H;
  let scale = 80;
  let offsetX = 0, offsetY = 0;
  let points = [];
  let pointHistory = [];

  let mode = 'initial';
  let animT = 0;
  let animStartTime = 0;
  const animDuration = 3200;

  let funcType = 'identity';

  let appMode = 'cauchy-theorem';

  let pointA = null;
  let isDraggingA = false;
  let aDragOffset = null;

  let pointers = new Map();
  let isPinching = false;
  let pinchState = null;
  let isPanning = false;
  let panStart = null, panOffsetStart = null;

  let circle = null;
  let circleDrawing = false;
  let circleDrawCenter = null;
  let circleDrawRadius = 0;
  let circleAnimating = false;
  let circleAnimStartTime = 0;
  const circleAnimDuration = 3500;
  let circleSamples = [];
  let circleIntegralResult = {re:0, im:0};
  let circleAnimCompleted = false;

  let flashState = null;
  const flashDuration = 3000;

  let panelDragging = false;
  let panelDragStart = null;
  let panelOffset = {x: 0, y: 0};
  let panelAttachComplex = null;
  let panelUserDragged = false;

  const funcDefs = {
    const1: {
      name: '1', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">1</span>',
      category: '🟢 全纯-整函-单值', categoryColor: '#4ade80',
      singularities: 'No singularities in ℂ',
      singularityPoints: [], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){ return {x:1, y:0}; }
    },
    identity: {
      name: 'z', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">z</span>',
      category: '🟢 全纯-整函-单值', categoryColor: '#4ade80',
      singularities: 'No singularities in ℂ',
      singularityPoints: [], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){ return {x:z.x, y:z.y}; }
    },
    z2p1: {
      name: 'z²+1', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">z</span><sup><span class="var">2</span></sup><span class="eq">+</span><span class="var">1</span>',
      category: '🟡 亚纯-∞极点-有理函-单值', categoryColor: '#facc15',
      singularities: 'z=∞: pole order 2',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=∞', order:2, isInf:true}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){ return {x:z.x*z.x-z.y*z.y+1, y:2*z.x*z.y}; }
    },
    inv_z: {
      name: '1/z', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">1</span><span class="eq">/</span><span class="var">z</span>',
      category: '🟡 亚纯-有限远极点-有理函-单值', categoryColor: '#facc15',
      singularities: 'z=0: simple pole',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=0', order:1}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const d = z.x*z.x + z.y*z.y;
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        return {x:z.x/d, y:-z.y/d};
      }
    },
    inv_z2: {
      name: '1/z²', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">1</span><span class="eq">/</span><span class="var">z</span><sup><span class="var">2</span></sup>',
      category: '🟡 亚纯-有限远极点-有理函-单值', categoryColor: '#facc15',
      singularities: 'z=0: pole order 2',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=0', order:2}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const d = z.x*z.x + z.y*z.y;
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        const x2 = z.x*z.x - z.y*z.y, y2 = 2*z.x*z.y;
        return {x:x2/(d*d), y:-y2/(d*d)};
      }
    },
    z_plus_invz: {
      name: 'z+1/z', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="eq">+</span><span class="var">1</span><span class="eq">/</span><span class="var">z</span>',
      category: '🟡 亚纯-混合极点-有理函-单值', categoryColor: '#facc15',
      singularities: 'z=0: simple pole; z=∞: simple pole',
      singularityPoints: [
        {x:0, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=0', order:1},
        {x:0, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=∞', order:1, isInf:true}
      ], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const d = z.x*z.x + z.y*z.y;
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        return {x:z.x+z.x/d, y:z.y-z.y/d};
      }
    },
    sinc: {
      name: 'sin(z)/z', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">sin</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">/</span><span class="var">z</span>',
      category: '🟡 亚纯-有限远可去-单值', categoryColor: '#facc15',
      singularities: 'z=0: removable singularity (lim=1)',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'removable', label:'z=0'}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const d = Math.hypot(z.x, z.y);
        if(d < 1e-6) return {x:1, y:0};
        const sz = {x:Math.sin(z.x)*Math.cosh(z.y), y:Math.cos(z.x)*Math.sinh(z.y)};
        return {x:(sz.x*z.x+sz.y*z.y)/(d*d), y:(sz.y*z.x-sz.x*z.y)/(d*d)};
      }
    },
    exp_z: {
      name: 'e^z', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">e</span><sup><span class="var">z</span></sup>',
      category: '🔴 非亚纯-∞本性奇点-单值', categoryColor: '#f87171',
      singularities: 'z=∞: essential singularity',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'essential', label:'z=∞', isInf:true}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const e = Math.exp(z.x);
        return {x:e*Math.cos(z.y), y:e*Math.sin(z.y)};
      }
    },
    sin_z: {
      name: 'sin(z)', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">sin</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span>',
      category: '🔴 非亚纯-∞本性奇点-单值', categoryColor: '#f87171',
      singularities: 'z=∞: essential singularity',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'essential', label:'z=∞', isInf:true}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        return {x:Math.sin(z.x)*Math.cosh(z.y), y:Math.cos(z.x)*Math.sinh(z.y)};
      }
    },
    exp_invz: {
      name: 'e^(1/z)', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">e</span><sup><span class="var">1</span><span class="eq">/</span><span class="var">z</span></sup>',
      category: '🔴 非亚纯-有限远本性奇点-单值', categoryColor: '#f87171',
      singularities: 'z=0: essential singularity',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'essential', label:'z=0'}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const d = z.x*z.x + z.y*z.y;
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        const rx = z.x/d, ry = -z.y/d;
        const e = Math.exp(rx);
        return {x:e*Math.cos(ry), y:e*Math.sin(ry)};
      }
    },
    sin_invz: {
      name: 'sin(1/z)', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">sin</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">1</span><span class="eq">/</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span>',
      category: '🔴 非亚纯-有限远本性奇点-单值', categoryColor: '#f87171',
      singularities: 'z=0: essential singularity',
      singularityPoints: [{x:0, y:0, type:'essential', label:'z=0'}], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const d = z.x*z.x + z.y*z.y;
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        const rx = z.x/d, ry = -z.y/d;
        return {x:Math.sin(rx)*Math.cosh(ry), y:Math.cos(rx)*Math.sinh(ry)};
      }
    },
    log_z: {
      name: 'log(z)', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">ln</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span>',
      category: '🔴 非亚纯-分支点-多值', categoryColor: '#f87171',
      singularities: 'z=0,∞: branch points; principal branch: arg∈(−π,π]',
      singularityPoints: [
        {x:0, y:0, type:'branch', label:'z=0'},
        {x:0, y:0, type:'branch', label:'z=∞', isInf:true}
      ], hasBranchCut: true,
      map: function(z){
        const d = Math.hypot(z.x, z.y);
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:-1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        return {x:Math.log(d), y:Math.atan2(z.y, z.x)};
      }
    },
    sqrt_z: {
      name: '√z', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">√</span><span class="var">z</span>',
      category: '🔴 非亚纯-分支点-多值', categoryColor: '#f87171',
      singularities: 'z=0,∞: branch points; principal branch: arg∈(−π,π]',
      singularityPoints: [
        {x:0, y:0, type:'branch', label:'z=0'},
        {x:0, y:0, type:'branch', label:'z=∞', isInf:true}
      ], hasBranchCut: true,
      map: function(z){
        const r = Math.hypot(z.x, z.y);
        if(r < 1e-10) return {x:0, y:0};
        const theta = Math.atan2(z.y, z.x)/2;
        const sr = Math.sqrt(r);
        return {x:sr*Math.cos(theta), y:sr*Math.sin(theta)};
      }
    },
    cuberoot_z: {
      name: 'z^(1/3)', latexHtml: '<span class="var">f</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="paren">)</span><span class="eq">=</span><span class="var">z</span><sup><span class="var">1</span><span class="eq">/</span><span class="var">3</span></sup>',
      category: '🔴 非亚纯-分支点-多值', categoryColor: '#f87171',
      singularities: 'z=0,∞: branch points; principal branch: arg∈(−π,π]',
      singularityPoints: [
        {x:0, y:0, type:'branch', label:'z=0'},
        {x:0, y:0, type:'branch', label:'z=∞', isInf:true}
      ], hasBranchCut: true,
      map: function(z){
        const r = Math.hypot(z.x, z.y);
        if(r < 1e-10) return {x:0, y:0};
        const theta = Math.atan2(z.y, z.x)/3;
        const cr = Math.pow(r, 1/3);
        return {x:cr*Math.cos(theta), y:cr*Math.sin(theta)};
      }
    }
  };

  function getCurrentFunc(){ return funcDefs[funcType]; }

  function resize(){
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    W = canvas.clientWidth || window.innerWidth || 300;
    H = canvas.clientHeight || window.innerHeight || 300;
    canvas.width = W * dpr;
    canvas.height = H * dpr;
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    render();
  }

  function toScreen(cx, cy){
    return {x: W/2 + offsetX + cx * scale, y: H/2 + offsetY - cy * scale};
  }
  function toComplex(sx, sy){
    return {x: (sx - W/2 - offsetX) / scale, y: -(sy - H/2 - offsetY) / scale};
  }

  const gridRange = 7.0;
  const gridStep = 0.5;
  const sampleStep = 0.04;
  const maxDisplay = 200;

  function mapPoint(z){
    if(mode === 'initial') return {x:z.x, y:z.y};
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    if(mode === 'animating'){
      const t = animT;
      const target = f.map(z);
      const res = {x: (1-t)*z.x + t*target.x, y: (1-t)*z.y + t*target.y};
      const mag = Math.hypot(res.x, res.y);
      if(mag > maxDisplay){ const k = maxDisplay/mag; return {x:res.x*k, y:res.y*k, inf:true}; }
      return res;
    }
    if(mode === 'reverseAnimating'){
      const t = animT;
      const target = f.map(z);
      const res = {x: (1-t)*z.x + t*target.x, y: (1-t)*z.y + t*target.y};
      const mag = Math.hypot(res.x, res.y);
      if(mag > maxDisplay){ const k = maxDisplay/mag; return {x:res.x*k, y:res.y*k, inf:true}; }
      return res;
    }
    const res = f.map(z);
    const mag = Math.hypot(res.x, res.y);
    if(mag > maxDisplay){ const k = maxDisplay/mag; return {x:res.x*k, y:res.y*k, inf:true}; }
    return res;
  }

  function isNearSingularity(zx, zy){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    for(const sp of f.singularityPoints){
      if(sp.isInf || sp.type === 'removable') continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(zx - sp.x, zy - sp.y);
      if(d < 0.08) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function mapCirclePoint(z){
    if(mode === 'initial') return {x:z.x, y:z.y};
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    let target;
    if(mode === 'animating'){
      const t = animT;
      target = f.map(z);
      if(target.inf) return target;
      target = {x: (1-t)*z.x + t*target.x, y: (1-t)*z.y + t*target.y};
    } else if(mode === 'reverseAnimating'){
      const t = animT;
      target = f.map(z);
      if(target.inf) return target;
      target = {x: (1-t)*z.x + t*target.x, y: (1-t)*z.y + t*target.y};
    } else {
      target = f.map(z);
    }
    if(target.inf || !isFinite(target.x) || !isFinite(target.y)) return {x:0, y:0, inf:true};
    const mag = Math.hypot(target.x, target.y);
    if(mag > 500) return {x:target.x, y:target.y, inf:true};
    return target;
  }

  function formatComplex(c, prec){
    prec = prec || 2;
    if(Math.abs(c.x) < Math.pow(10, -prec) && Math.abs(c.y) < Math.pow(10, -prec)) return '0';
    let s = '';
    if(Math.abs(c.x) >= Math.pow(10, -prec)){
      s += (c.x >= 0 ? '+' : '') + c.x.toFixed(prec);
    }
    if(Math.abs(c.y) >= Math.pow(10, -prec)){
      s += (c.y >= 0 ? '+' : '') + c.y.toFixed(prec) + 'i';
    }
    return s || '0';
  }

  function getEnclosedSingularityInfo(){
    if(!circle) return null;
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    const enclosed = [];
    for(const sp of f.singularityPoints){
      if(sp.type === 'removable') continue;
      if(sp.isInf){
        const distFromOrigin = Math.hypot(circle.cx, circle.cy);
        if(circle.r > 5 && distFromOrigin < circle.r * 0.5){
          enclosed.push(sp);
        }
        continue;
      }
      const d = Math.hypot(sp.x - circle.cx, sp.y - circle.cy);
      if(d < circle.r - 1e-6) enclosed.push(sp);
    }
    if(enclosed.length === 0) return null;
    return enclosed;
  }

  function circleEnclosesPointA(){
    if(!circle || !pointA) return false;
    const d = Math.hypot(pointA.x - circle.cx, pointA.y - circle.cy);
    return d < circle.r - 1e-6;
  }

  function computeResidue(funcType, sp){
    if(sp.type === 'removable') return {x:0, y:0};
    if(sp.type === 'branch') return null;
    if(sp.type === 'essential'){
      if(funcType === 'exp_invz' && sp.label === 'z=0') return {x:1, y:0};
      if(funcType === 'sin_invz' && sp.label === 'z=0') return {x:1, y:0};
      return null;
    }
    if(sp.type === 'pole'){
      switch(funcType){
        case 'inv_z': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:1, y:0} : null;
        case 'inv_z2': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:0, y:0} : null;
        case 'z_plus_invz': return sp.label === 'z=0' ? {x:1, y:0} : null;
        case 'z2p1': return {x:0, y:0};
        case 'sinc': return {x:0, y:0};
        default: return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  function drawCircle(){
    if(!circle && !circleDrawing) return;
    const cx = circle ? circle.cx : circleDrawCenter.x;
    const cy = circle ? circle.cy : circleDrawCenter.y;
    const r = circle ? circle.r : circleDrawRadius;
    if(r < 0.05) return;

    let alpha = 0.75;
    let color = '0, 255, 200';
    if(flashState && circle){
      const elapsed = performance.now() - flashState.startTime;
      const flashT = elapsed / flashDuration;
      if(flashT < 1){
        const blink = Math.sin(flashT * Math.PI * 8) * 0.5 + 0.5;
        alpha = 0.3 + blink * 0.7;
        color = '255, 60, 60';
      }
    }

    const n = 360;
    ctx.beginPath();
    let first = true;
    let prevS = null;
    for(let k = 0; k <= n; k++){
      const t = 2 * Math.PI * k / n;
      const zx = cx + r * Math.cos(t);
      const zy = cy + r * Math.sin(t);
      if(isNearSingularity(zx, zy)){ first = true; prevS = null; continue; }
      const p = mapCirclePoint({x: zx, y: zy});
      if(p.inf || !isFinite(p.x) || !isFinite(p.y)){ first = true; prevS = null; continue; }
      const s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
      if(s.x < -5000 || s.x > W+5000 || s.y < -5000 || s.y > H+5000){ first = true; prevS = s; continue; }
      if(first){ ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); first = false; }
      else { ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y); }
      prevS = s;
    }
    if(!first){
      ctx.closePath();
      if(circleDrawing){
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 255, 200, 0.35)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      } else {
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(' + color + ', ' + alpha + ')';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
        if(!flashState) ctx.setLineDash([6, 4]);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
    }

    const centerP = mapCirclePoint({x: cx, y: cy});
    if(isFinite(centerP.x) && isFinite(centerP.y)){
      const centerS = toScreen(centerP.x, centerP.y);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(centerS.x, centerS.y, 4, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle = flashState ? '#ff4444' : '#0ff';
      ctx.fill();
      if(circleDrawing){
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,200,0.7)';
        ctx.font = '12px monospace';
        ctx.textAlign = 'left';
        ctx.fillText('r = ' + r.toFixed(2), centerS.x + 10, centerS.y - 10);
      }
    }
  }

  function drawPhoton(){
    if(!circleAnimating && !circleAnimCompleted) return null;
    let idx;
    if(circleAnimating){
      const elapsed = performance.now() - circleAnimStartTime;
      let progress = Math.min(1, elapsed / circleAnimDuration);
      idx = Math.min(circleSamples.length - 1, Math.floor(progress * (circleSamples.length - 1)));
    } else {
      idx = circleSamples.length - 1;
    }
    if(idx < 0 || idx >= circleSamples.length) return null;

    const z = circleSamples[idx].z;
    const mapped = mapPoint(z);
    if(!isFinite(mapped.x) || !isFinite(mapped.y)) return null;
    const s = toScreen(mapped.x, mapped.y);

    for(let i = 4; i >= 0; i--){
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 5 + i * 5, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 240, 80, ' + (0.12 - i * 0.02) + ')';
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 5, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffeb3b';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.stroke();

    return s;
  }

  function drawPointA(){
    if((appMode !== 'cauchy-formula' && appMode !== 'taylor' && appMode !== 'laurent') || !pointA) return;
    const s = toScreen(pointA.x, pointA.y);

    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 7, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#0ff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.stroke();

    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.font = 'bold 13px monospace';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('a', s.x, s.y - 12);

    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula'){
      const fa = getCurrentFunc().map(pointA);
      if(isFinite(fa.x) && isFinite(fa.y)){
        const faStr = formatComplex(fa, 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,200,0.85)';
        ctx.font = '10px monospace';
        ctx.fillText('f(a)=' + faStr, s.x, s.y + 20);
      }
    }
  }

  function startCircleAnimation(startAngle){
    if(!circle) return;
    circleAnimating = true;
    circleAnimCompleted = false;
    circleAnimStartTime = performance.now();
    circleSamples = [];
    circleIntegralResult = {re: 0, im: 0};

    const n = 200;
    for(let k = 0; k <= n; k++){
      const t = startAngle + 2 * Math.PI * k / n;
      const zx = circle.cx + circle.r * Math.cos(t);
      const zy = circle.cy + circle.r * Math.sin(t);
      const p = mapCirclePoint({x: zx, y: zy});
      let s = null;
      if(isFinite(p.x) && isFinite(p.y)){
        s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
      }
      circleSamples.push({z: {x: zx, y: zy}, screen: s});
    }

    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
    hintLabel.style.display = 'none';
    requestAnimationFrame(circleAnimLoop);
  }

  function circleAnimLoop(now){
    if(!circleAnimating) return;
    const elapsed = now - circleAnimStartTime;
    let progress = elapsed / circleAnimDuration;
    if(progress > 1) progress = 1;

    const nTotal = circleSamples.length - 1;
    const currentIdx = Math.min(nTotal, Math.floor(progress * nTotal));

    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    let sumRe = 0, sumIm = 0;

    if(appMode === 'cauchy-theorem' || appMode === 'residue'){
      for(let k = 0; k < currentIdx; k++){
        const s1 = circleSamples[k];
        const s2 = circleSamples[k+1];
        const dzRe = s2.z.x - s1.z.x;
        const dzIm = s2.z.y - s1.z.y;
        const mzx = (s1.z.x + s2.z.x) / 2;
        const mzy = (s1.z.y + s2.z.y) / 2;
        const fz = f.map({x: mzx, y: mzy});
        if(fz.inf) continue;
        sumRe += fz.x * dzRe - fz.y * dzIm;
        sumIm += fz.x * dzIm + fz.y * dzRe;
      }
      circleIntegralResult = {re: sumRe, im: sumIm};
    } else {
      for(let k = 0; k < currentIdx; k++){
        const s1 = circleSamples[k];
        const s2 = circleSamples[k+1];
        const dzRe = s2.z.x - s1.z.x;
        const dzIm = s2.z.y - s1.z.y;
        const mzx = (s1.z.x + s2.z.x) / 2;
        const mzy = (s1.z.y + s2.z.y) / 2;

        const fz = f.map({x: mzx, y: mzy});
        if(fz.inf) continue;

        const denomRe = mzx - pointA.x;
        const denomIm = mzy - pointA.y;
        const denomMag = denomRe*denomRe + denomIm*denomIm;
        if(denomMag < 1e-10) continue;

        const intRe = (fz.x*denomRe + fz.y*denomIm) / denomMag;
        const intIm = (fz.y*denomRe - fz.x*denomIm) / denomMag;

        sumRe += intRe * dzRe - intIm * dzIm;
        sumIm += intRe * dzIm + intIm * dzRe;
      }
      circleIntegralResult = {
        re: sumIm / (2 * Math.PI),
        im: -sumRe / (2 * Math.PI)
      };
    }

    const curZ = circleSamples[currentIdx].z;
    const mapped = mapCirclePoint(curZ);
    let s = circleSamples[currentIdx].screen;
    if(isFinite(mapped.x) && isFinite(mapped.y)){
      s = toScreen(mapped.x, mapped.y);
      circleSamples[currentIdx].screen = s;
    }

    updateIntegralFollower(s, circleIntegralResult.re, circleIntegralResult.im, progress < 1);
    render();

    if(progress < 1){
      requestAnimationFrame(circleAnimLoop);
    } else {
      circleAnimating = false;
      circleAnimCompleted = true;
      integralFollower.classList.add('drag-enabled');
      if(appMode === 'cauchy-theorem'){
        const enclosed = getEnclosedSingularityInfo();
        updateIntegralFollowerFinal(s, circleIntegralResult.re, circleIntegralResult.im, !!enclosed);
      } else if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula'){
        const fa = getCurrentFunc().map(pointA);
        const err = Math.hypot(circleIntegralResult.re - fa.x, circleIntegralResult.im - fa.y);
        const enclosed = getEnclosedSingularityInfo();
        const enclosesA = circleEnclosesPointA();
        updateIntegralFollowerFinalFormula(s, circleIntegralResult.re, circleIntegralResult.im, fa, err, enclosesA, !!enclosed);
      } else if(appMode === 'residue'){
        const enclosed = getEnclosedSingularityInfo();
        updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue(s, circleIntegralResult.re, circleIntegralResult.im, enclosed);
      }
      panelAttachComplex = {x: curZ.x, y: curZ.y};
      panelUserDragged = false;
      render();
    }
  }

  function updateIntegralFollower(photonScreen, re, im, isAnimating){
    let valStr;
    if(Math.abs(re) < 0.0005 && Math.abs(im) < 0.0005){
      valStr = '0';
    } else {
      const reStr = (re >= 0 ? '+' : '') + re.toFixed(2);
      const imStr = (im >= 0 ? '+' : '') + im.toFixed(2) + 'i';
      valStr = reStr + ' ' + imStr;
    }

    if(appMode === 'cauchy-theorem' || appMode === 'residue'){
      integralFormula.innerHTML = '<span style="font-size:18px; font-style:italic;">∮</span><span class="int-c">C</span><span style="font-style:italic;">f(z)dz</span> ' + (isAnimating ? '≈' : '=');
      integralValue.textContent = valStr;
      integralValue.className = '';
    } else {
      let fracHtml = '<span style="display:inline-block; text-align:center; vertical-align:middle; line-height:1.1; margin-right:2px;">';
        fracHtml += '<span style="display:block; border-bottom:1px solid #0ff; padding:0 2px; font-size:11px;">1</span>';
        fracHtml += '<span style="display:block; font-size:11px;">2πi</span></span>';
        integralFormula.innerHTML = fracHtml + '∮<span class="int-c">C</span><span style="font-style:italic;">f(z)/(z-a)dz</span> ' + (isAnimating ? '≈' : '=');
      integralValue.textContent = valStr;
      integralValue.className = '';
    }

    if(photonScreen){
      integralFollower.style.left = (photonScreen.x + 14) + 'px';
      integralFollower.style.top = (photonScreen.y - 52) + 'px';
      integralFollower.style.transform = 'none';
    }
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
  }

  function updateIntegralFollowerFinal(photonScreen, re, im, encloses){
    let valStr;
    if(Math.abs(re) < 0.005 && Math.abs(im) < 0.005){
      valStr = '0';
    } else {
      const reStr = (re >= 0 ? '+' : '') + re.toFixed(2);
      const imStr = (im >= 0 ? '+' : '') + im.toFixed(2) + 'i';
      valStr = reStr + ' ' + imStr;
    }
    let suffix = '';
    if(encloses){
      suffix = ' <span style="color:#f87171;">(encloses singularity)</span>';
    } else {
      suffix = ' <span style="color:#4ade80;">(analytic region)</span>';
    }
    integralFormula.innerHTML = '<span style="font-size:18px; font-style:italic;">∮</span><span class="int-c">C</span><span style="font-style:italic;">f(z)dz</span> = ' + valStr + suffix;
    integralValue.textContent = '';
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
  }

  function updateIntegralFollowerFinalFormula(photonScreen, intRe, intIm, fa, err, enclosesA, hasOtherSingularity){
    const intStr = formatComplex({x:intRe, y:intIm}, 2);

    let html = '<div style="font-size:13px; font-style:italic; white-space:nowrap;">';
    let fracHtml2 = '<span style="display:inline-block; text-align:center; vertical-align:middle; line-height:1.1; margin-right:2px;">';
    fracHtml2 += '<span style="display:block; border-bottom:1px solid #0ff; padding:0 2px; font-size:11px;">1</span>';
    fracHtml2 += '<span style="display:block; font-size:11px;">2πi</span></span>';
    html += fracHtml2 + '∮<span class="int-c">C</span>f(z)/(z-a)dz = ' + intStr;
    if(enclosesA && !hasOtherSingularity){
      const faStr = formatComplex(fa, 2);
      const errStr = err < 0.005 ? '✓ matches f(a)' : 'err = ' + err.toFixed(4);
      html += '<div style="margin-top:5px; color:#4ade80; font-size:12px; font-family:monospace;">f(a) = ' + faStr + ' &nbsp;|&nbsp; ' + errStr + '</div>';
    } else if(!enclosesA){
      html += ' <span style="color:#facc15;">(C does not enclose a)</span>';
    } else if(hasOtherSingularity){
      html += ' <span style="color:#f87171;">(f not analytic)</span>';
    }
    html += '</div>';
    integralFormula.innerHTML = html;
    integralValue.textContent = '';
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
  }

  function updateIntegralFollowerFinalResidue(photonScreen, re, im, enclosed){
    let valStr = formatComplex({x:re, y:im}, 2);
    let html = '<div style="font-size:13px; font-style:italic; white-space:nowrap;">';
    html += '<span style="font-size:18px; font-style:italic;">∮</span><span class="int-c">C</span><span style="font-style:italic;">f(z)dz</span> = ' + valStr;
    html += '</div>';
    if(enclosed && enclosed.length > 0){
      let sumRes = {x:0, y:0};
      let resDetails = [];
      for(const sp of enclosed){
        const res = computeResidue(funcType, sp);
        if(res){
          sumRes.x += res.x;
          sumRes.y += res.y;
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=' + formatComplex(res, 2));
        } else {
          resDetails.push(sp.label + ': Res=∞/unknown');
        }
      }
      const twoPiIRes = {x: -2*Math.PI*sumRes.y, y: 2*Math.PI*sumRes.x};
      html += '<div style="margin-top:4px; color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;">ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(sumRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style="color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;">2πi·ΣRes = ' + formatComplex(twoPiIRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style="margin-top:3px; color:#aaa; font-size:10px; font-family:monospace;">' + resDetails.join(' | ') + '</div>';
    } else {
      html += '<div style="margin-top:4px; color:#4ade80; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;">No poles inside C → ΣRes = 0</div>';
    }
    integralFormula.innerHTML = html;
    integralValue.textContent = '';
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
  }

  function getConvergenceInfo(funcType, a){
    const ra = Math.hypot(a.x, a.y);
    switch(funcType){
      case 'const1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = 1', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'identity': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = a + (z−a)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z2p1': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = (a²+1) + 2a(z−a) + (z−a)²', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z': 
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−1</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = Σ (−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−1</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'inv_z2':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−2</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = Σ (n+1)(−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−2</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'z_plus_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z + z<sup>−1</sup>', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'f(z) = z + Σ (−1)<sup>n</sup> a<sup>−n−1</sup> (z−a)<sup>n</sup>', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sinc': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'Taylor at 0: 1 − z²/3! + z⁴/5! − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = e<sup>a</sup> · Σ (z−a)<sup>n</sup>/n!', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sin_z': return {radius: Infinity, series: 'f(z) = sin(a) + cos(a)(z−a) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'exp_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: Σ z<sup>−n</sup>/n!', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'sin_invz':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Laurent at 0: z<sup>−1</sup> − z<sup>−3</sup>/3! + ...', type: 'laurent'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'Essential singularity at 0', type: 'laurent'};
      case 'log_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'ln(a) + Σ (−1)<sup>n+1</sup>(z−a)<sup>n</sup>/(n·a<sup>n</sup>)', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'sqrt_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: '√a + (z−a)/(2√a) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      case 'cuberoot_z':
        if(ra < 1e-6) return {radius: 0, series: 'Branch point — no convergent series', type: 'none'};
        return {radius: ra, series: 'a<sup>1/3</sup> + (z−a)/(3a<sup>2/3</sup>) − ...', type: 'taylor'};
      default: return {radius: 0, series: 'Unknown', type: 'none'};
    }
  }

  function drawGrid(){
    ctx.fillStyle = '#000000';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 255, 200, 0.45)';

    for(let y = -gridRange; y <= gridRange + 0.001; y += gridStep){
      ctx.beginPath();
      let first = true;
      for(let x = -gridRange; x <= gridRange + 0.001; x += sampleStep){
        if(isNearSingularity(x, y)){ first = true; continue; }
        const p = mapPoint({x, y});
        if(p.inf){ first = true; continue; }
        const s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
        const out = s.x < -200 || s.x > W+200 || s.y < -200 || s.y > H+200;
        if(first){ if(!out){ ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); first = false; } }
        else if(!out){ ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y); }
        else { first = true; }
      }
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    for(let x = -gridRange; x <= gridRange + 0.001; x += gridStep){
      ctx.beginPath();
      let first = true;
      for(let y = -gridRange; y <= gridRange + 0.001; y += sampleStep){
        if(isNearSingularity(x, y)){ first = true; continue; }
        const p = mapPoint({x, y});
        if(p.inf){ first = true; continue; }
        const s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
        const out = s.x < -200 || s.x > W+200 || s.y < -200 || s.y > H+200;
        if(first){ if(!out){ ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); first = false; } }
        else if(!out){ ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y); }
        else { first = true; }
      }
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 255, 200, 0.65)';

    ctx.beginPath();
    let first = true;
    for(let x = -gridRange; x <= gridRange + 0.001; x += sampleStep){
      if(isNearSingularity(x, 0)){ first = true; continue; }
      const p = mapPoint({x, y: 0});
      const s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
      const out = s.x < -200 || s.x > W+200 || s.y < -200 || s.y > H+200;
      if(first){ if(!out){ ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); first = false; } }
      else if(!out){ ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y); }
      else { first = true; }
    }
    ctx.stroke();

    ctx.beginPath();
    first = true;
    for(let y = -gridRange; y <= gridRange + 0.001; y += sampleStep){
      if(isNearSingularity(0, y)){ first = true; continue; }
      const p = mapPoint({x: 0, y});
      const s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
      const out = s.x < -200 || s.x > W+200 || s.y < -200 || s.y > H+200;
      if(first){ if(!out){ ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); first = false; } }
      else if(!out){ ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y); }
      else { first = true; }
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  function drawBranchCut(){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    if(!f.hasBranchCut || mode === 'initial') return;
    const origin = toScreen(0, 0);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(origin.x, origin.y);
    ctx.lineTo(origin.x - Math.max(W,H)*2, origin.y);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 60, 60, 0.3)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.setLineDash([5, 4]);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
  }

  function drawSingularities(){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    for(const sp of f.singularityPoints){
      if(sp.isInf) continue;
      const s = toScreen(sp.x, sp.y);
      if(s.x < -50 || s.x > W+50 || s.y < -50 || s.y > H+50) continue;
      const r = 8;
      switch(sp.type){
        case 'pole':
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff4444'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x-r, s.y-r); ctx.lineTo(s.x+r, s.y+r); ctx.stroke();
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x+r, s.y-r); ctx.lineTo(s.x-r, s.y+r); ctx.stroke();
          break;
        case 'essential':
          ctx.fillStyle = '#aa44ff';
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y-r); ctx.lineTo(s.x+r*0.6, s.y+r*0.3); ctx.lineTo(s.x-r*0.6, s.y+r*0.3); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y+r); ctx.lineTo(s.x+r*0.6, s.y-r*0.3); ctx.lineTo(s.x-r*0.6, s.y-r*0.3); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
          break;
        case 'branch':
          ctx.fillStyle = '#ff8800';
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y-r); ctx.lineTo(s.x+r, s.y); ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y+r); ctx.lineTo(s.x-r, s.y); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
          break;
        case 'removable':
          ctx.fillStyle = '#44ff44';
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 5, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
          break;
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff9999';
      ctx.font = '10px monospace';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      let labelText = sp.label || '';
      if(sp.type === 'pole' && sp.order){
        labelText += ' (order ' + sp.order + ')';
      }
      ctx.fillText(labelText, s.x, s.y - r - 6);
    }
  }

  function drawPoints(){
    for(let i = 0; i < points.length; i++){
      const p = points[i];
      const wz = mapPoint(p);
      const s = toScreen(wz.x, wz.y);
      const r = 5;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, r + 4, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color + '20'; ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, r + 2, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color + '50'; ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, r, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5; ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff'; ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.45)'; ctx.font = '10px monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      const rx = Math.abs(p.x) < 0.001 ? 0 : p.x;
      const ry = Math.abs(p.y) < 0.001 ? 0 : p.y;
      const origLabel = (rx >= 0 ? '+' : '') + rx.toFixed(2) + (ry >= 0 ? '+' : '') + ry.toFixed(2) + 'i';
      ctx.fillText(origLabel, s.x, s.y - r - 4);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
      if(wz.inf){
        ctx.fillText('→ ∞', s.x, s.y - r - 16);
      } else {
        const mx = Math.abs(wz.x) < 0.001 ? 0 : wz.x;
        const my = Math.abs(wz.y) < 0.001 ? 0 : wz.y;
        const mappedLabel = '→ ' + (mx >= 0 ? '+' : '') + mx.toFixed(2) + (my >= 0 ? '+' : '') + my.toFixed(2) + 'i';
        ctx.fillText(mappedLabel, s.x, s.y - r - 16);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.font = 'bold 11px monospace';
      ctx.fillText('z' + (i+1), s.x, s.y + r + 12);
    }
  }

  function updateLabels(){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula'){
      let base = f.latexHtml;
      let rhs = base.replace(/.*?<span class="eq">=<\/span>/, '');
      funcLabel.innerHTML = rhs + '<span class="eq">/</span><span class="paren">(</span><span class="var">z</span><span class="eq">-</span><span class="var">a</span><span class="paren">)</span>';
    } else if(appMode === 'taylor'){
      funcLabel.innerHTML = f.latexHtml + '<span style="color:#4ade80; font-size:14px;"> &nbsp;Taylor at a</span>';
    } else if(appMode === 'laurent'){
      funcLabel.innerHTML = f.latexHtml + '<span style="color:#facc15; font-size:14px;"> &nbsp;Laurent at a</span>';
    } else {
      funcLabel.innerHTML = f.latexHtml;
    }
    categoryLabel.textContent = f.category;
    categoryLabel.style.color = f.categoryColor;
    singularityLabel.textContent = f.singularities;
  }

  function updateTriggerText(){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    triggerText.innerHTML = f.latexHtml;
  }

  function updateHint(){
    if(circleAnimating || flashState) return;
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-theorem'){
      hintLabel.textContent = 'Drag on grid to draw a circle → auto computes ∮C f(z)dz';
    } else if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula'){
      if(!pointA){
        hintLabel.textContent = 'Tap to place point a';
      } else if(!circle){
        hintLabel.textContent = 'Drag from a to draw circle around a';
      } else {
        hintLabel.textContent = '';
      }
    } else if(appMode === 'residue'){
      hintLabel.textContent = 'Drag to draw circle enclosing poles → computes Σ Res(f, a_k)';
    } else if(appMode === 'taylor' || appMode === 'laurent'){
      if(!pointA){
        hintLabel.textContent = 'Tap to place expansion center a';
      } else {
        hintLabel.textContent = 'Drag to pan • Wheel to zoom';
      }
    }
  }

  function render(){
    drawGrid();
    drawBranchCut();
    drawSingularities();
    drawCircle();
    drawPhoton();
    drawPointA();
    drawPoints();
    
    if(circleAnimCompleted && panelAttachComplex && !panelUserDragged && !panelDragging){
      const s = toScreen(panelAttachComplex.x, panelAttachComplex.y);
      integralFollower.style.left = (s.x + 14) + 'px';
      integralFollower.style.top = (s.y - 52) + 'px';
      integralFollower.style.transform = 'none';
    }
    
    if((appMode === 'taylor' || appMode === 'laurent') && pointA){
      const info = getConvergenceInfo(funcType, pointA);
      const convR = info.radius;
      if(convR > 0 && convR < 1000){
        const s = toScreen(pointA.x, pointA.y);
        const sr = convR * scale;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, sr, 0, Math.PI*2);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(100, 255, 100, 0.4)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.setLineDash([]);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(100,255,100,0.6)';
        ctx.font = '10px monospace';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillText('R = ' + (convR === Infinity ? '∞' : convR.toFixed(2)), s.x, s.y - sr - 6);
      }
      integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
      integralFormula.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px; color:#0ff; white-space:nowrap; line-height:1.4;">' + info.series + '</div><div style="font-size:11px; color:#4ade80; margin-top:3px; font-family:monospace;">R = ' + (info.radius === Infinity ? '∞' : info.radius.toFixed(2)) + '</div>';
      integralValue.textContent = '';
      const s2 = toScreen(pointA.x, pointA.y);
      integralFollower.style.left = (s2.x + 14) + 'px';
      integralFollower.style.top = (s2.y - 52) + 'px';
      integralFollower.style.transform = 'none';
    }
  }

  function flashLoop(now){
    if(!flashState) return;
    const elapsed = now - flashState.startTime;
    if(elapsed < flashDuration){
      render();
      requestAnimationFrame(flashLoop);
    } else {
      circle = null;
      flashState = null;
      flashPanel.style.display = 'none';
      updateHint();
      render();
    }
  }

  function startFlash(enclosed, reasonType){
    if(reasonType === 'no-a'){
      flashState = {startTime: performance.now(), duration: flashDuration};
      flashText.innerHTML = "The contour does <b>not enclose</b> point <i>a</i>.<br>Cauchy integral formula requires C to surround a.";
      flashReason.textContent = '∮C f(z)/(z-a)dz = 0 (Cauchy theorem)';
      flashPanel.style.display = 'block';
      requestAnimationFrame(flashLoop);
      return;
    }
    if(!enclosed || enclosed.length === 0) return;
    flashState = {startTime: performance.now(), duration: flashDuration};

    let reasons = [];
    for(const sp of enclosed){
      if(sp.type === 'pole'){
        reasons.push(sp.label + ': pole of order ' + (sp.order || 1));
      } else if(sp.type === 'essential'){
        reasons.push(sp.label + ': essential singularity');
      } else if(sp.type === 'branch'){
        reasons.push(sp.label + ': branch point');
      }
    }

    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula'){
      flashText.innerHTML = "The contour encloses singularities where <i>f(z)</i> is <b>not analytic</b>.<br>Cauchy integral formula does not apply.";
    } else {
      flashText.innerHTML = "The contour encloses singularities where <i>f(z)</i> is <b>not analytic</b>.<br>Cauchy's theorem does not apply.";
    }
    flashReason.textContent = reasons.join(' | ');
    flashPanel.style.display = 'block';

    requestAnimationFrame(flashLoop);
  }

  function doReset(){
    mode = 'initial';
    scale = 80; offsetX = 0; offsetY = 0;
    points = []; pointHistory = [];
    circle = null;
    circleDrawing = false;
    circleAnimating = false;
    circleAnimCompleted = false;
    circleSamples = [];
    flashState = null;
    flashPanel.style.display = 'none';
    integralFollower.style.display = 'none';
    integralFollower.classList.remove('drag-enabled');
    animBtn.textContent = 'Animate';
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' || appMode === 'taylor' || appMode === 'laurent') pointA = null;
    panelAttachComplex = null;
    panelUserDragged = false;
    updateLabels();
    updateHint();
    render();
  }

  resetBtn.addEventListener('click', doReset);

  modeSwitch.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
      if(this.classList.contains('active')) return;
      modeSwitch.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
      this.classList.add('active');
      appMode = this.getAttribute('data-mode');
      doReset();
      if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' || appMode === 'residue' || appMode === 'taylor' || appMode === 'laurent'){
        pointA = null;
      }
      updateHint();
    });
  });

  funcTrigger.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    e.stopPropagation();
    funcOptions.classList.toggle('active');
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function(){
    funcOptions.classList.remove('active');
  });
  funcOptions.querySelectorAll('div[data-value]').forEach(opt => {
    opt.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      e.stopPropagation();
      funcOptions.classList.remove('active');
      funcType = this.getAttribute('data-value');
      updateTriggerText();
      doReset();
      if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula'){
        pointA = null;
      }
      updateHint();
    });
  });

  function hitTestPointA(sx, sy){
    if(!pointA) return false;
    const s = toScreen(pointA.x, pointA.y);
    return Math.hypot(s.x - sx, s.y - sy) < 20;
  }

  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    if(circleAnimating || mode === 'animating' || mode === 'reverseAnimating' || flashState) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    try { canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch(err) {}

    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA && hitTestPointA(e.clientX, e.clientY)){
      isDraggingA = true;
      aDragOffset = {x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY};
      pointers.set(e.pointerId, {x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, isA: true});
      return;
    }

    const c = toComplex(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    pointers.set(e.pointerId, {
      x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
      downX: e.clientX, downY: e.clientY, time: Date.now(),
      cx: c.x, cy: c.y
    });

    if(pointers.size > 1){
      isPinching = true;
      circleDrawing = false;
      const vals = Array.from(pointers.values()).filter(p => !p.isA);
      if(vals.length >= 2){
        const d0 = vals[0], d1 = vals[1];
        const dist = Math.hypot(d0.x - d1.x, d0.y - d1.y);
        const center = {x: (d0.x + d1.x)/2, y: (d0.y + d1.y)/2};
        pinchState = {
          lastDist: dist, lastCenter: center,
          lastScale: scale, lastOffsetX: offsetX, lastOffsetY: offsetY
        };
      }
    } else {
      if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
        circleDrawCenter = {x: pointA.x, y: pointA.y};
      }
    }
  });

  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
    if(circleAnimating || mode === 'animating' || mode === 'reverseAnimating' || flashState) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if(!pointers.has(e.pointerId)) return;

    const p = pointers.get(e.pointerId);

    if(p.isA && isDraggingA && pointA){
      const c = toComplex(e.clientX, e.clientY);
      pointA.x = c.x;
      pointA.y = c.y;
      render();
      return;
    }

    const moved = Math.hypot(e.clientX - p.downX, e.clientY - p.downY);
    pointers.set(e.pointerId, {...p, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, moved});

    if(pointers.size === 2 && isPinching){
      const vals = Array.from(pointers.values()).filter(p => !p.isA);
      if(vals.length >= 2){
        const d0 = vals[0], d1 = vals[1];
        const dist = Math.hypot(d0.x - d1.x, d0.y - d1.y);
        const center = {x: (d0.x + d1.x)/2, y: (d0.y + d1.y)/2};
        if(pinchState && pinchState.lastDist > 0){
          const zoomFactor = dist / pinchState.lastDist;
          const newScale = Math.max(5, Math.min(5000, pinchState.lastScale * zoomFactor));
          const complexAtLast = {
            x: (pinchState.lastCenter.x - W/2 - pinchState.lastOffsetX) / pinchState.lastScale,
            y: -(pinchState.lastCenter.y - H/2 - pinchState.lastOffsetY) / pinchState.lastScale
          };
          const newOffsetX = center.x - W/2 - complexAtLast.x * newScale;
          const newOffsetY = center.y - H/2 + complexAtLast.y * newScale;
          if(isFinite(newScale) && isFinite(newOffsetX) && isFinite(newOffsetY)){
            scale = newScale; offsetX = newOffsetX; offsetY = newOffsetY;
            render();
          }
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    if(pointers.size === 1 && !isPinching && !isPanning && !isDraggingA && mode === 'initial' && !circle){
      if(appMode === 'taylor' || appMode === 'laurent'){
        // no circle drawing in these modes
      } else if(moved > 12 && !circleDrawing){
        circleDrawing = true;
        if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
          circleDrawCenter = {x: pointA.x, y: pointA.y};
        } else {
          circleDrawCenter = {x: p.cx, y: p.cy};
        }
        render();
      }
      if(circleDrawing){
        const cur = toComplex(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
          circleDrawRadius = Math.hypot(cur.x - pointA.x, cur.y - pointA.y);
        } else {
          circleDrawRadius = Math.hypot(cur.x - circleDrawCenter.x, cur.y - circleDrawCenter.y);
        }
        render();
        return;
      }
    }

    if(isPanning && panStart && panOffsetStart){
      offsetX = panOffsetStart.x + (e.clientX - panStart.x);
      offsetY = panOffsetStart.y + (e.clientY - panStart.y);
      render();
    }
  });

  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e){
    if(circleAnimating || mode === 'animating' || mode === 'reverseAnimating' || flashState) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const p = pointers.get(e.pointerId);
    pointers.delete(e.pointerId);

    if(p && p.isA){
      isDraggingA = false;
      if(pointers.size === 0){
        isPinching = false;
        pinchState = null;
      }
      return;
    }

    if(circleDrawing){
      circleDrawing = false;
      if(circleDrawRadius > 0.1 && circleDrawCenter){
        const endComplex = toComplex(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        const startAngle = Math.atan2(endComplex.y - circleDrawCenter.y, endComplex.x - circleDrawCenter.x);
        circle = { cx: circleDrawCenter.x, cy: circleDrawCenter.y, r: circleDrawRadius, active: true };

        if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula'){
          const enclosesA = circleEnclosesPointA();
          if(!enclosesA){
            startFlash(null, 'no-a');
          } else {
            const enclosed = getEnclosedSingularityInfo();
            if(enclosed && enclosed.length > 0){
              startFlash(enclosed);
            } else {
              startCircleAnimation(startAngle);
            }
          }
        } else if(appMode === 'residue'){
          const enclosed = getEnclosedSingularityInfo();
          const hasBranch = enclosed && enclosed.some(sp => sp.type === 'branch');
          if(hasBranch){
            startFlash(enclosed);
          } else {
            startCircleAnimation(startAngle);
          }
        } else if(appMode === 'taylor' || appMode === 'laurent'){
          // no circle animation in these modes
        } else {
          const enclosed = getEnclosedSingularityInfo();
          if(enclosed && enclosed.length > 0){
            startFlash(enclosed);
          } else {
            startCircleAnimation(startAngle);
          }
        }
      }
      pointers.clear();
      isPinching = false;
      pinchState = null;
      isPanning = false;
      panStart = null;
      panOffsetStart = null;
      render();
      return;
    }

    if((appMode === 'cauchy-formula' || appMode === 'taylor' || appMode === 'laurent') && !pointA && p && !isPinching && !isPanning){
      const dt = Date.now() - p.time;
      const dist = Math.hypot(p.x - p.downX, p.y - p.downY);
      if(dt < 350 && dist < 12){
        const c = toComplex(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        pointA = {x: c.x, y: c.y};
        updateHint();
        render();
      }
    }

    if(appMode === 'cauchy-theorem' && pointers.size === 0 && !isPinching && !isPanning && p){
      const dt = Date.now() - p.time;
      const dist = Math.hypot(p.x - p.downX, p.y - p.downY);
      if(dt < 350 && dist < 12){
        if(points.length < 8){
          pointHistory.push(points.slice());
          const c = toComplex(e.clientX, e.clientY);
          points.push({x: c.x, y: c.y, color: colors[points.length % colors.length]});
          render();
        }
      }
    }

    if(pointers.size === 0){
      isPinching = false;
      pinchState = null;
      isPanning = false;
      panStart = null;
      panOffsetStart = null;
    }
  });

  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', function(e){
    const p = pointers.get(e.pointerId);
    pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
    if(p && p.isA) isDraggingA = false;
    if(pointers.size === 0){
      isPinching = false;
      pinchState = null;
      isPanning = false;
      panStart = null;
      panOffsetStart = null;
    }
    if(circleDrawing){ circleDrawing = false; render(); }
  });

  canvas.addEventListener('wheel', function(e){
    if(circleAnimating || mode === 'animating' || mode === 'reverseAnimating' || flashState) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const mouseComplex = toComplex(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    const factor = e.deltaY > 0 ? 0.85 : 1.15;
    const newScale = Math.max(5, Math.min(5000, scale * factor));
    const newOffsetX = e.clientX - W/2 - mouseComplex.x * newScale;
    const newOffsetY = e.clientY - H/2 + mouseComplex.y * newScale;
    if(isFinite(newScale) && isFinite(newOffsetX) && isFinite(newOffsetY)){
      scale = newScale; offsetX = newOffsetX; offsetY = newOffsetY;
      render();
    }
  }, {passive: false});

  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e){
    if(circleAnimating || mode === 'animating' || mode === 'reverseAnimating' || flashState) return;
    if(e.button === 1 || e.shiftKey){
      isPanning = true;
      panStart = {x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY};
      panOffsetStart = {x: offsetX, y: offsetY};
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e){
    if(isPanning && panStart && panOffsetStart){
      offsetX = panOffsetStart.x + (e.clientX - panStart.x);
      offsetY = panOffsetStart.y + (e.clientY - panStart.y);
      render();
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', function(){
    isPanning = false;
    panStart = null;
    panOffsetStart = null;
  });

  function startAnim(){
    if(mode !== 'initial' || circleAnimating || flashState) return;
    mode = 'animating';
    animT = 0;
    animStartTime = performance.now();
    animBtn.textContent = 'Animating...';
    requestAnimationFrame(animLoop);
  }

  function startReverseAnim(){
    if(mode !== 'interactive' || circleAnimating || flashState) return;
    mode = 'reverseAnimating';
    animT = 1;
    animStartTime = performance.now();
    animBtn.textContent = 'Reversing...';
    requestAnimationFrame(reverseAnimLoop);
  }

  function animLoop(now){
    if(mode !== 'animating') return;
    const elapsed = now - animStartTime;
    animT = Math.min(1, elapsed / animDuration);
    render();
    if(animT < 1){
      requestAnimationFrame(animLoop);
    } else {
      mode = 'interactive';
      animBtn.textContent = 'Animate';
      render();
      if(circle && circle.active){
        setTimeout(function(){
          if(mode === 'interactive' && circle && circle.active){
            startCircleAnimation(0);
          }
        }, 50);
      }
    }
  }

  function reverseAnimLoop(now){
    if(mode !== 'reverseAnimating') return;
    const elapsed = now - animStartTime;
    const rawT = Math.min(1, elapsed / animDuration);
    animT = 1 - rawT;
    render();
    if(rawT < 1){
      requestAnimationFrame(reverseAnimLoop);
    } else {
      mode = 'initial';
      animBtn.textContent = 'Animate';
      render();
      if(circle && circle.active){
        setTimeout(function(){
          if(mode === 'initial' && circle && circle.active){
            startCircleAnimation(0);
          }
        }, 50);
      }
    }
  }

  animBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){
    if(circleAnimating || flashState) return;
    if(mode === 'initial'){
      startAnim();
    } else if(mode === 'interactive'){
      startReverseAnim();
    }
  });

  integralFollower.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    if(!circleAnimCompleted && appMode !== 'taylor' && appMode !== 'laurent') return;
    e.stopPropagation();
    e.preventDefault();
    panelDragging = true;
    panelUserDragged = true;
    panelDragStart = {x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY};
    const rect = integralFollower.getBoundingClientRect();
    panelOffset = {x: rect.left, y: rect.top};
    try { integralFollower.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch(err) {}
  });
  integralFollower.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
    if(!panelDragging) return;
    e.stopPropagation();
    e.preventDefault();
    const dx = e.clientX - panelDragStart.x;
    const dy = e.clientY - panelDragStart.y;
    integralFollower.style.left = (panelOffset.x + dx) + 'px';
    integralFollower.style.top = (panelOffset.y + dy) + 'px';
    integralFollower.style.transform = 'none';
  });
  integralFollower.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e){
    panelDragging = false;
    panelDragStart = null;
  });
  integralFollower.addEventListener('pointercancel', function(e){
    panelDragging = false;
    panelDragStart = null;
  });

  updateTriggerText();
  updateLabels();
  updateHint();
  resize();
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

复制后保存为 `cauchy.html`，双击即可在浏览器中打开。如果你想让我用 widget 再展示一遍修复后的交互效果来验证，告诉我。

## Kimi

改进圆点映射动画阈值与极点标记

用户上传了一张截图，显示的是复平面网格变形工具在 Cauchy Formula 模式下的情况。函数是 sin(1/z)，点 a 被放置在靠近原点（z=0 本性奇点）的位置，围道 C 似乎包围了 a 但也很靠近 z=0。用户的问题是：如何避免 z 靠近 a 时柯西核函数在这里疯狂跳跃？从截图来看：

1. 函数是 sin(1/z)，在 z=0 处有本性奇点
2. 点 a（白色圆点）被放置在靠近原点的位置
3. 围道 C（虚线圆）包围了 a，但可能也很靠近 z=0
4. 在 z 靠近 a 时，柯西核 1/(z-a) 的分母趋近于 0，导致数值爆炸
5. 同时由于 sin(1/z) 在 z=0 附近有极端振荡，两者叠加导致网格/积分路径出现"疯狂跳跃"

用户想知道如何避免这种现象。这涉及到数值计算中的奇点处理问题。我需要分析：

1. 现象原因：柯西核 1/(z-a) 在 z→a 时的奇异性
2. 叠加 sin(1/z) 在 z→0 时的本性奇点行为
3. 数值积分时的采样点如果太靠近 a 或 0，会导致巨大的数值跳跃

解决方案：

1. **自适应采样**：在靠近 a 的区域增加采样密度，避免跳过奇点
2. **正则化/截断**：在 z 非常靠近 a 时，对核函数进行数值截断或正则化处理
3. **围道远离奇点**：确保围道 C 与 a 保持一定距离，同时也远离其他奇点（如 z=0）
4. **使用主值积分**：如果必须靠近，使用 Cauchy 主值
5. **变形围道**：在数值计算时，将围道稍微变形避开过于接近的区域
6. **对数尺度可视化**：在显示时使用对数尺度，避免视觉上的"跳跃"
7. **排除奇异邻域**：在采样时，如果 |z-a| < ε，跳过该点或使用极限值

对于我们的具体实现：

- 当前代码在 `circleAnimLoop` 中计算积分时，已经有一个 `denomMag < 1e-10` 的检查来跳过太靠近 a 的点
- 但在绘制网格（`drawGrid`）和绘制围道映射（`drawCircle`）时，没有类似的保护
- 在 `mapPoint` 和 `mapCirclePoint` 中，对于 sin(1/z)，当 z 靠近 0 时，函数值会剧烈振荡并趋向无穷

具体修复建议：

1. **在网格绘制中增加核函数保护**：当计算 f(z)/(z-a) 用于显示时，如果 |z-a| < ε，不绘制或截断
2. **在围道积分采样中更精细地处理**：当前使用 200 个采样点，在靠近 a 的地方应该自适应加密
3. **增加最小距离检查**：在绘制变形后的围道时，如果映射点跳变太大，断开路径（已有部分实现）
4. **对 Cauchy Formula 模式下的网格显示进行特殊处理**：显示的是 f(z)/(z-a) 的映射，而不仅仅是 f(z)

等等，让我仔细看代码。在 Cauchy Formula 模式下，函数标签显示的是 sin(1/z)/(z-a)，但代码中的 `mapPoint` 仍然只映射 f(z)，而不是 f(z)/(z-a)。实际上，在 Cauchy Formula 模式下，网格应该显示的是被积函数 f(z)/(z-a) 的映射，而不是 f(z) 的映射。这是关键问题！在 Cauchy Formula 模式下，用户看到的网格应该是 g(z) = f(z)/(z-a) 的映射，这样围道积分 ∮ g(z) dz 才有几何意义。但当前代码在所有模式下都映射 f(z)，只有在积分计算时才除以 (z-a)。所以"疯狂跳跃"有两个原因：

1. 网格显示的是 f(z) 而不是 f(z)/(z-a)，所以用户看不到核函数的影响
2. 当 a 靠近奇点时，f(z)/(z-a) 的奇异性叠加

但用户的问题是"如何避免 z 靠近 a 时柯西核函数在这里疯狂跳跃"，这更侧重于数值和视觉处理。让我给出全面的分析和解决方案：**原因分析：**

1. 柯西核 1/(z-a) 在 z=a 处有一阶极点，|z-a|→0 时 |1/(z-a)|→∞
2. sin(1/z) 在 z=0 处有本性奇点，在任意靠近 0 的邻域内无限振荡
3. 当 a 靠近 z=0 时，两个奇点叠加：f(z)/(z-a) 在 z=a 处有极点，在 z=0 处有本性奇点
4. 数值采样时，如果采样点恰好非常靠近 a，映射点会跳到很远的地方
5. 网格绘制时，连续的采样点之间如果有一个靠近 a，连线就会横跨整个屏幕

**解决方案：**对于我们的代码，具体可以：

1. **Cauchy Formula 模式下网格映射应包含核函数**：修改 `mapPoint` 在 `cauchy-formula` 模式下返回 `f(z)/(z-a)` 而非 `f(z)`，这样用户看到的才是被积函数的真实形态。
2. **在 mapPoint 中增加核函数保护**：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
    const d = Math.hypot(z.x - pointA.x, z.y - pointA.y);
    if(d < 0.02) return {x:0, y:0, inf:true}; // 标记为无穷，绘图时断开
    // 或者进行数值截断
}
```

实际上，对于 `drawGrid`，如果我们在每个点都计算 f(z)/(z-a)，性能会受影响，因为 grid 有很多点。但应该还好。让我开始写代码。由于代码很长，我需要小心地修改。关键修改点：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function mapIntegrand(z){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    let fz = f.map(z);
    if(fz.inf) return fz;
    
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
        const dx = z.x - pointA.x;
        const dy = z.y - pointA.y;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
        if(d2 < 1e-8) return {x:0, y:0, inf:true};
        // fz / (z-a) = fz * conj(z-a) / |z-a|^2
        return {
            x: (fz.x*dx + fz.y*dy) / d2,
            y: (fz.y*dx - fz.x*dy) / d2
        };
    }
    return fz;
}
```

然后在 `drawGrid` 中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function drawGrid(){
    // ...
    const useIntegrand = (appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA);
    
    for(let y = -gridRange; ...){
        // ...
        const p = useIntegrand ? mapIntegrand({x, y}) : mapPoint({x, y});
        // ...
    }
}
```

但 `mapPoint` 还处理动画插值（animating/reverseAnimating）。对于网格显示，在 cauchy-formula 模式下，我们可能也想看动画。这变得复杂。简化方案：

- 保持 `mapPoint` 不变（它处理动画插值）
- 新增一个包装函数 `getMappedPoint(z)`，在 interactive/initial 模式下根据 appMode 选择是否应用核函数

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function getDisplayMap(z){
    if(mode === 'animating' || mode === 'reverseAnimating' || mode === 'initial'){
        // 动画和初始状态显示 f(z) 的映射
        return mapPoint(z);
    }
    // interactive 模式下，根据 appMode 决定
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
        const fz = getCurrentFunc().map(z);
        if(fz.inf) return fz;
        const dx = z.x - pointA.x;
        const dy = z.y - pointA.y;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
        if(d2 < 1e-6) return {x:0, y:0, inf:true};
        return {
            x: (fz.x*dx + fz.y*dy)/d2,
            y: (fz.y*dx - fz.x*dy)/d2
        };
    }
    return mapPoint(z);
}
```

我觉得围道动画显示被积函数映射更有教育意义。让我们试试。另外，对于 `isNearSingularity`，在 cauchy-formula 模式下，a 点应该被视为奇点：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function isNearSingularity(zx, zy){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    for(const sp of f.singularityPoints){
        if(sp.isInf || sp.type === 'removable') continue;
        const d = Math.hypot(zx - sp.x, zy - sp.y);
        if(d < 0.08) return true;
    }
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
        const d = Math.hypot(zx - pointA.x, zy - pointA.y);
        if(d < 0.08) return true;
    }
    return false;
}
```

- 修改 `mapPoint` 末尾，在返回前检查 appMode
- 修改 `mapCirclePoint` 同样处理

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function mapPoint(z){
    // ... existing code ...
    let res;
    if(mode === 'initial') res = {x:z.x, y:z.y};
    else if(mode === 'animating') { /* ... */ }
    else if(mode === 'reverseAnimating') { /* ... */ }
    else res = f.map(z);
    
    // Apply Cauchy kernel if in formula mode
    if((mode === 'interactive' || mode === 'initial') && appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA && !res.inf){
        const dx = z.x - pointA.x;
        const dy = z.y - pointA.y;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
        if(d2 < 1e-8) return {x:0, y:0, inf:true};
        const x = (res.x*dx + res.y*dy)/d2;
        const y = (res.y*dx - res.x*dy)/d2;
        const mag = Math.hypot(x, y);
        if(mag > maxDisplay) return {x:x*maxDisplay/mag, y:y*maxDisplay/mag, inf:true};
        return {x, y};
    }
    return res;
}
```

但这样在 animating 模式下不会应用核函数，因为 mode === 'animating' 时提前返回了。这正好：动画展示 f(z) 的映射，完成后进入 interactive 模式，自动显示 f(z)/(z-a)。这个方案很好！同样修改 `mapCirclePoint`。对于 `isNearSingularity`，加入 pointA 检查，这样网格在 a 点周围断开。还有一个问题：在 `drawGrid` 中，网格线穿过 a 点时，由于 `isNearSingularity` 返回 true，会断开，这很好。让我确认 `isNearSingularity` 在 `drawGrid` 中的使用。是的，在 x-loop 和 y-loop 中都调用了。现在考虑 `drawCircle`。在 Cauchy Formula 模式下，`drawCircle` 使用 `mapCirclePoint`，如果应用了核函数，围道靠近 a 时会映射到很远的地方。这其实是正确的数学行为——极点映射到无穷远。但为了让动画可见，我们需要截断。`mapCirclePoint` 中已有截断逻辑（mag > 500）。如果应用核函数后 mag > 500，返回 inf，drawCircle 会断开。这可能导致围道在靠近 a 的地方出现缺口。更好的做法是在 `drawCircle` 中，对于 inf 点不画线，保持连续。当前逻辑已经是这样：如果 p.inf，则 first = true（断开）。所以围道会在靠近 a 的地方断开，这是可以接受的。现在考虑 `circleAnimLoop` 中的积分计算。它已经在计算 f(z)/(z-a)，并且有 denomMag < 1e-10 的保护。我们可以把这个阈值放宽到 1e-6 或根据步长自适应。另外，截图显示 a 点靠近 z=0 本性奇点。对于 sin(1/z)，z=0 附近的网格本身就一团糟。这是无法避免的数学事实。我们只能建议用户不要把 a 点放在太靠近 z=0 的地方。好，让我现在写完整的修复代码。我会基于 v61 的代码进行修改。主要修改点：

1. `isNearSingularity`：加入 pointA 检查
2. `mapPoint`：在末尾加入核函数应用（仅 interactive/initial 模式）
3. `mapCirclePoint`：同样加入核函数应用

让我开始编码。

问题根源在于：**柯西核 1/(z−a) 在 z=a 处有一阶极点**，而你的截图里 a 又放得极其靠近 z=0 这个**本性奇点**——两个奇点叠加，导致被积函数 f(z)/(z−a) 在数值采样时只要稍微靠近 a 就会爆炸到无穷远，画布上的连线就会"疯狂跳跃"横跨屏幕。我给出**三层修复**：数值截断 + 网格断开 + 自适应禁区，然后重新生成文件。

### 修复思路

| 层级 | 问题 | 修复 |
| --- | --- | --- |

| **1. 数学层** | 1/(z−a) 在 z=a 处极点 | Cauchy Formula 模式下，网格与围道**直接显示被积函数** f(z)/(z−a) ，让用户看到极点在哪 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **2. 数值层** | ∥z−a∥<ε 时除零爆炸 | 设置**禁区半径** ε=0.06 ，靠近 a 时标记 `inf`，绘图自动断开连线 |
| **3. 视觉层** | a 点不可见，用户不知道碰了极点 | a 点绘制**红色叉号**（极点标记），与 z=0 的紫色星形区分 |

### 关键代码修改

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
// 1. 禁区检测：把 pointA 也当作"奇点"处理，网格在这里自动断开
function isNearSingularity(zx, zy){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    for(const sp of f.singularityPoints){
        if(sp.isInf || sp.type === 'removable') continue;
        const d = Math.hypot(zx - sp.x, zy - sp.y);
        if(d < 0.08) return true;
    }
    // 新增：Cauchy Formula 模式下，a 点是核函数的极点
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA){
        const d = Math.hypot(zx - pointA.x, zy - pointA.y);
        if(d < 0.06) return true;   // 禁区半径
    }
    return false;
}

// 2. 映射函数：Cauchy Formula 模式下返回 f(z)/(z-a) 而非 f(z)
function mapPoint(z){
    // ... 原有动画插值逻辑不变 ...
    let res = /* 原有 f.map(z) 结果 */;
    
    // 新增：interactive / initial 模式下叠加柯西核
    if((mode === 'interactive' || mode === 'initial') 
       && appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA && !res.inf){
        const dx = z.x - pointA.x;
        const dy = z.y - pointA.y;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
        if(d2 < 1e-8) return {x:0, y:0, inf:true};  // 截断
        const x = (res.x*dx + res.y*dy)/d2;
        const y = (res.y*dx - res.x*dy)/d2;
        const mag = Math.hypot(x, y);
        if(mag > maxDisplay) return {x:x*maxDisplay/mag, y:y*maxDisplay/mag, inf:true};
        return {x, y};
    }
    return res;
}

// 3. 围道积分采样：用相对阈值，避免靠近 a 时跳变
// 在 circleAnimLoop 中，把 denomMag < 1e-10 改为：
const denomMag = denomRe*denomRe + denomIm*denomIm;
if(denomMag < 1e-6) continue;  // 更宽松的禁区，跳过而非爆炸

// 4. a 点视觉标记：绘制红色叉（极点）
function drawPointA(){
    // ... 原有白色圆点 ...
    // 新增：红色叉表示这是柯西核的极点
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff4444'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x-6, s.y-6); ctx.lineTo(s.x+6, s.y+6); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x+6, s.y-6); ctx.lineTo(s.x-6, s.y+6); ctx.stroke();
}
```

### 额外建议

对于 sin(1/z) 这种在 z=0 有**本性奇点**的函数，无论怎么数值保护， z=0 邻域内都是无限振荡的。建议：

- **不要把 a 放在 z=0 附近**（本性奇点邻域内没有 Laurent 展开的主部概念，数值上必然混乱）
- 使用 `1/z` 或 `e^(1/z)` 测试 Cauchy Formula 时，把 a 放在远离原点的位置（如 a=1+0.5i ），围道半径取 0.3 左右，效果最干净

用户上传了两张截图，展示了动画过程中的两个瞬间。他注意到在动画过程中，曲线形状之间似乎有一个"瞬间跳跃"。让我分析一下这两张图：图1：Animate 按钮显示 "Animating..."，说明动画正在进行中。网格显示的是 sin(1/z) 的映射，a 点在左上方，围道是一个虚线圆。积分面板显示公式和结果。图2：动画似乎已经完成（按钮显示 "Animate"），但围道还在，积分面板显示结果 -0.14 - 0.21i。用户问的是：是不是我的设计让动画在这两个曲线形状之间有瞬间跳跃？让我仔细看：图1中，围道映射后的形状看起来比较"正常"，是一个变形的圆。
图2中，围道映射后的形状变得非常复杂，有很多交叉和环。等等，让我重新理解。用户说的是"这两个曲线形状之间"——可能是指：

1. 动画过程中（图1）和动画结束后（图2）围道映射的形状差异

但仔细看，图1显示的是动画中（Animating...），图2显示的是动画后。在图1中，围道映射看起来是一个相对平滑的变形圆。在图2中，围道映射变得非常复杂，有很多自交。这实际上是因为：

- 图1（动画中）：animT 在 0 到 1 之间，mapPoint 做的是插值 `(1-t)*z + t*f(z)`。由于 sin(1/z) 在 z=0 附近有本性奇点，插值过程中靠近原点的点会被拉得很远，但因为是插值，所以变化是连续的。
- 图2（动画后/交互模式）：mode = 'interactive'，mapPoint 直接返回 f(z)，也就是 sin(1/z) 的完整映射。这时围道如果包围了 z=0，那么映射后的曲线会极其复杂，因为 sin(1/z) 在 z=0 附近无限振荡。

但用户说的"瞬间跳跃"可能不是这个。让我再看...实际上，用户可能是在说：当动画从 animating 切换到 interactive 的那一刻，围道的形状发生了突变。因为在 animating 模式下，mapCirclePoint 也使用 animT 插值；而动画结束后切换到 interactive 模式，mapCirclePoint 直接返回 f.map(z)（对于 Cauchy Formula 还会叠加核函数）。等等，让我仔细看代码。在 v62 中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function mapCirclePoint(z){
    if(mode === 'initial') return {x:z.x, y:z.y};
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    let target;
    if(mode === 'animating'){
        const t = animT;
        target = f.map(z);
        if(target.inf) return target;
        target = {x: (1-t)*z.x + t*target.x, y: (1-t)*z.y + t*target.y};
    } else if(mode === 'reverseAnimating'){
        // ...
    } else {
        target = f.map(z);
    }
    // ... 然后对于 cauchy-formula 叠加核函数
    if(appMode === 'cauchy-formula' && pointA && mode !== 'animating' && mode !== 'reverseAnimating'){
        target = applyCauchyKernel(target, z);
    }
```

关键问题在这里！当 `mode === 'animating'` 时：

- `target = f.map(z)` 先计算 sin(1/z)
- 然后插值 `(1-t)*z + t*target`
- **不叠加柯西核**（因为 mode === 'animating'）

当动画结束，`mode` 变成 `'interactive'` 的那一刻：

- `target = f.map(z)` 计算 sin(1/z)
- **叠加柯西核** `applyCauchyKernel(target, z)`（因为 mode !== 'animating'）

所以，在动画的最后一帧（t=1），围道显示的是 `sin(1/z)` 的插值结果（此时插值结果就是 sin(1/z)）。
但动画结束后的第一帧，围道显示的是 `sin(1/z)/(z-a)`。这就是"瞬间跳跃"的原因！从 `sin(1/z)` 突然变成 `sin(1/z)/(z-a)`。这确实是设计问题。对于 Cauchy Formula 模式，用户期望看到的是被积函数 `f(z)/(z-a)` 的映射，但动画过程中显示的是 `f(z)` 的映射，动画结束后突然切换到 `f(z)/(z-a)`。修复方案：
在 Cauchy Formula 模式下，动画过程中也应该叠加柯西核，这样动画的终点（t=1）和 interactive 模式看到的就是同一个东西。但等等，这样动画过程中就会看到极点附近的跳变，可能更混乱。另一个方案：在 Cauchy Formula 模式下，动画不执行（或者动画显示的就是被积函数的插值）。让我想想最好的方案...实际上，用户的截图显示：

- 图1（动画中）：围道是一个相对简单的变形圆，这是 `sin(1/z)` 的插值映射
- 图2（动画后）：围道变得非常复杂，有很多环，这是 `sin(1/z)/(z-a)` 的映射

因为 a 点不在原点，所以 `1/(z-a)` 引入了一个新的极点。在 z=a 附近，被积函数爆炸。由于围道包围了 z=0（本性奇点）但不包围 z=a（假设），所以 `sin(1/z)` 在围道上是有定义的，但 `sin(1/z)/(z-a)` 在围道上也是有定义的（只要围道不经过 a）。等等，看图2中围道似乎经过了 a 点附近？不，a 点在左上方，围道中心似乎在右下方...让我重新理解用户的截图。图1：

- a 点在左上方（白色圆点）
- 围道（虚线）中心似乎也在左上方，包围了 a 点？
